# network_write native-rejections live cases — partial checkpoint, 2026-10-28

**Functional probe observations recorded; Task 7 is incomplete.** The maintainer first stopped at the L3 silent-rename finding, then reopened the disposable project to observe the remaining probes. The agreed next phase is joint investigation of uncovered issues before any timing measurements. No ruling to keep or change the silent-rename behavior has been made. The earlier stop and interrupted attempt are preserved below.

Candidate: branch `refactor/network-write-native-rejections`, source `f1ae806954a65a44f7638226a42cd9c66de70a8a`, installed package `4.2.0-local.76.gf1ae806`. The host and worker matched the local Release publish by SHA-256. Portal UI language: English, confirmed by the maintainer. Full mode; item/document budgets both 1,000,000, matching the [Task 0 baseline](https://github.com/Czarnak/tia-portal-mcp/blob/docs-archive-2026-10-30/docs/superpowers/acceptance/reports/inputs/2026-10-27-network-write-timing-before.md).

Initial full hardware equaled the original fixture (95 devices, 14 root devices, 108 nodes, four subnets); the project was open and unmodified. Raw MCP responses, requests, exact matching audit-v2 records and provenance from the first session remain in the local ignored directory `artifacts/network-native/task7-20261028/`; the resumed directory is identified below. Relevant canonical documents are retained directly, with labeled projections for complete hardware/status captures. Full Task 7 acceptance and replay fixtures remain incomplete.

`compile_check` compiles PLC software. Its silence does not establish hardware compiler acceptance. Every attempted failure must be inspected before retrying; trusted means the native call completed without rejection, not that requested values were read back.

Portal rejected the create with `engineering_rejected`, `EngineeringTargetInvocationException` and the native message. A filtered hardware read found the existing device, with the original subnets. The existing PLC software compiled with zero errors/warnings; the requested new CPU software was absent. Close without save, reopen and clean status succeeded.

## L3 — colliding subnet rename: O3 silent drop; decision deferred

The request renamed Ethernet subnet `3547-1` from `PN/IE_2` to the existing name `PN/IE_1`. The call succeeded and verification was `trusted`, but both the mutation result and the independent landed read retained `PN/IE_2`. PLC software compilation reported zero errors/warnings. This is evidence of an ignored rename; it is not evidence that the requested name landed.

The maintainer chose to stop and return later while watching TIA itself. No `postcondition_failed` read-back, collision precheck or other product change was authorized or implemented. Close without save, reopen and clean status succeeded.

Request:

```json
{"operations":[{"operationId":"l3-rename","operation":"update_subnet","target":{"kind":"subnet","subnetId":"3547-1"},"subnetChanges":{"name":"PN/IE_1"}}]}
```

## L5 — earlier interrupted attempt, no acceptance outcome

L5 had begun when the maintainer closed Portal. It returned `binding_conflict` because complete worker/Portal identity was unavailable. Subsequent landed/compile/cleanup calls were denied on the invalidated binding. This does not prove whether a setter was reached. The client exited; no retry or replay was performed in that stopped session. The later authorized L5 attempt is recorded separately below.

- On resume, verify candidate provenance and fixture state again, have the maintainer present, and advance one case at a time with a stop boundary before the next case. Do not rerun already accepted captures without a reason or treat the interrupted L5 attempt as acceptance evidence.

### L5 resumed — duplicate PROFINET name: accepted and landed; software O3

With automatic naming disabled on the selected GSD device, Portal accepted `PnDeviceName:"mot01"`. The independent landed check reported `mot01` on both selected peers; the changed device had auto-generation `false`, and the peer remained `true`. This is an accepted collision, not a silent drop. PLC software compilation reported zero errors/warnings; hardware compilation was not observed.

Close without save, reopen and clean status succeeded. The restoration status reported the original project open and unmodified. Exact lifecycle documents remain in `L5-restore-close/open/status.json` in the resumed capture directory.

### L6 — manual PROFINET name with automatic naming enabled: O1

Portal threw `EngineeringNotSupportedException` for `set_PnDeviceName`. The item therefore returned `worker_operation_failed`, with the native `engineeringError` preserved, an empty typed `appliedSettings`, and no verification entries. The landed check retained auto-generation `true` and the original generated name. Software compilation reported zero errors/warnings.

Close without save, reopen and clean status succeeded. The restoration status reported the original project open and unmodified. Exact lifecycle documents remain in `L6-restore-close/open/status.json` in the resumed capture directory.

```json
{"operations":[{"operationId":"l6-pn","operation":"configure_network_device","target":{"kind":"node","deviceName":"S7-1500/ET200MP station_2","nodeId":"E1","interfacePath":[{"name":"PLC_2/SlashTest","positionNumber":1},{"name":"PROFINET interface_2","positionNumber":33024}]},"changes":{"pnDeviceName":"zz-native-l6-name"}}]}
```

### L11 attempt A — partial configure stops at unsupported SubnetMask: O1

A separate setup disabled automatic naming on GSD device A, and a setup read confirmed auto-generation `false` with its original `wen_m201` name. The observed L11 item then requested Address, SubnetMask and an invalid PROFINET name. It stopped earlier than intended: `set_SubnetMask` raised `EngineeringNotSupportedException`, surfaced as `worker_operation_failed`. This attempt does not exercise the intended PN-name rejection or the intended two-key applied subset.

The typed failure retained only `Address:"192.168.111.77"`. The independent landed read confirmed that address, the original `255.255.255.0` mask and original `wen_m201` name; auto-generation remained `false` from setup. The later create item was skipped. Software compilation reported zero errors/warnings. Close without save, reopen and clean status succeeded. The separate CPU-target attempt L11b below tests the intended Address+Mask then PN rejection; its outcome must not be conflated with this observation.

### L11b — CPU target accepts all three settings and later create: software O3

A separate CPU-target setup disabled automatic naming, and its landed read retained the original Address, Mask and generated name with auto-generation now `false`. The subsequent item accepted all requested settings: `Address:"192.168.203.42"`, `SubnetMask:"255.255.254.0"` and the literal `PnDeviceName:"Invalid PN Name!"`. The independent landed read confirmed all three values. The later create item also succeeded; the landed hardware contained `ZZ_L11b_Skipped` as subnet `590-5`. The word `Skipped` in that temporary name is not an item status.

Both operations were succeeded/trusted, and PLC software compilation reported zero errors/warnings. This is an accepted-and-landed observation, not the intended rejected-PN partial-write case and not a silent drop. Hardware compilation was not observed. Close without save, reopen and clean status succeeded. Neither L11 attempt demonstrates a rejection after exactly Address+Mask: attempt A rejected Mask after Address, while this CPU attempt accepted PN and continued. A literal rejected-PN fixture cannot be claimed from these captures; that plan assumption remains for the joint issue review.

### L12 — five-operation lifecycle: writes accepted; O2 software diagnostics

All five ordered writes succeeded: create a separate subnet, add a CPU, connect the existing CPU node to known subnet `3547-1`, rename that subnet, then delete it. The known connected subnet is deliberately separate from the newly created subnet: its native ID is available to initial planning, and its original connections provide the delete info guard. This preserves the five operation kinds without pretending that a new native subnet ID was known before creation.

Verification was `trusted` for four items and `superseded` for the rename followed by deletion. The landed hardware showed the added `ZZ_L12_CPU`, kept new subnet `ZZ_L12_Created` (`590-5`), and no longer contained deleted subnet `3547-1`. The connected-delete info guard listed the five initially connected nodes; it did not include the CPU node connected earlier in this same call. That is the retained initial-snapshot limitation, not a claim that every final consequence was replanned.

This was not compile-clean acceptance: after the batch, `PLC_Wash` software compilation reported **3 errors / 0 warnings**, with undefined tags `WEN_M101~Head`, `WEN_M201~Head` and `MOT01~Head` in `CommunicationContainer_FB (FB300)`. The other two compiled PLCs (`PLC_2/SlashTest` and `ZZ_L12_CPU`) reported zero errors/warnings. The three-error count is the compiler's total; hierarchy and summary messages are not extra errors. Close without save, reopen and clean status succeeded, discarding all temporary changes.

### L13 — five-item batch stops on the third item: O1 with prior writes retained

The create-subnet and add-CPU items succeeded. The third item replaced L12's connect with malformed Address `not-an-ip`; Portal rejected it with `EngineeringTargetInvocationException` / `engineering_rejected`, retaining an empty typed configure outcome. Rename and delete were `skipped` with `earlierOperationFailed`. The root retained `success:false`, `error:null` and MCP `isError:false`; verification contained only the two earlier trusted items.

The landed check confirmed the original CPU address and original `PN/IE_2` subnet name, while the earlier created `ZZ_L13_CPU` and `ZZ_L13_Created` subnet remained. Those observations demonstrate stop-at-first without rollback of the earlier successful items. The created CPU's software compile reported zero errors/warnings. Close without save, reopen and clean status succeeded.
